<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C89BE88B-D6EA-4F33-A8C3-496597C6989A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Four of these numbers can make two pairs so that each pair adds up to $5871$. Which number is the odd one out?

**A** $1917$

**B** $2743$ 

**C** $3008$ 

**D** $3954$

**E** $2863$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Two pairs are $=1917, 3954$ and $3008, 2863$

Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1917 + 3954 \\\\
&= 5871 \\\\
&= 3008 + 2863 \\\\
&= 5871
\end{aligned}
$

Odd one $= 2743$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$2743$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Two pairs are $=1917, 3954$ and $3008, 2863$
<emptyline>
Let's verify.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1917 + 3954 \\\\
&= 5871 \\\\
&= 3008 + 2863 \\\\
&= 5871
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Odd one $= 2743$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>B</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2743$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-BCC6F2CB-8539-4FD4-9E74-FEBEFF999875'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find $2000 - 12 + 20 - 12$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2000 - 12 + 20 - 12 \\\\
&= 2000 - 12 + 8 \\\\
&= 2000 - 4 \\\\
&= 1996
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1996$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2000 - 12 + 20 - 12 \\\\
&= 2000 - 12 + 8 \\\\
&= 2000 - 4 \\\\
&= 1996
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1996$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7DCC3D45-5CB3-4A24-A48F-06B6CED96443'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the average of $\frac {1}{2}$ and $\frac {1}{3}$ ?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (\dfrac{1} {2} + \dfrac {1} {3}) \div 2 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{3} {2 \times 3} + \dfrac {2} {3 \times 2}) \div 2 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{3} {6} + \dfrac {2} {6}) \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {6} \div {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {6 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {12}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac{5} {12}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= (\dfrac{1} {2} + \dfrac {1} {3}) \div 2 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{3} {2 \times 3} + \dfrac {2} {3 \times 2}) \div 2 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{3} {6} + \dfrac {2} {6}) \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {6} \div {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {6 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{5} {12}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac{5} {12}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-57C9979B-2ACE-4BAF-8E7F-AC51C6373882'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Exactly one of these statements is correct. Which one?

**A** $43^2+ 56^2 = 4981$        

**B** $55^2 + 66^2= 7387$

**C** $77^2+ 22^2 = 6415$   

**D** $44^2+ 63^2 = 5905$   

**E** $34^2 + 51^2 = 3756$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A}    &= 43^2+ 56^2 \\\\
            &= 1849 + 3136 \\\\
            &= \red
{4985} \neq 4981 \\\\
\text{B}    &= 55^2 + 66^2 \\\\
            &= 3025 + 4356 \\\\
            &= \red
{7381} \neq 7387 \\\\
\text{C}    &= 77^2+ 22^2 \\\\
            &= 5929 + 484 \\\\
            &= \red
{6413}  \neq 6415 \\\\
\text{D}    &= 44^2+ 63^2 \\\\
            &= 1936 + 3969 \\\\
            &= \green
{5905}
\end{aligned}
$

Correct statement $= \text{D}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

D

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{A}    &= 43^2+ 56^2 \\\\
            &= 1849 + 3136 \\\\
            &= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{4985} \neq 4981 \\\\
\text{B}    &= 55^2 + 66^2 \\\\
            &= 3025 + 4356 \\\\
            &= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{7381} \neq 7387 \\\\
\text{C}    &= 77^2+ 22^2 \\\\
            &= 5929 + 484 \\\\
            &= \rtq_katex_color_wrong_answer{6413}  \neq 6415 \\\\
\text{D}    &= 44^2+ 63^2 \\\\
            &= 1936 + 3969 \\\\
            &= \rtq_katex_color_answer{5905}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Correct statement $= \text{D}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

Circle **TWO** numbers from this list, which **multiply** together to 
give $1$ million $(=1 000 000)$.

$2,\:\:\:5 000,\:\:\:50 000,\:\:\:2 000,\:\:\:200,\:\:\:50,\:\:\:20$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$5000, 200 \ \text {or} \ 50000, 20$

Lets verify it

$
\begin{aligned}
5000, 200   &= 5000 \times 200 \\\\
         &= 1000000 \\\\
50000, 20   &= 50000 \times 20 \\\\
         &= 1000000
\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5A02BFDB-4654-4CDA-8AB3-4762789C5789'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Circle the fraction from this list, which is closest to $1$.

$\frac {5} {7},\:\:\:\frac {2} {3},\:\:\:\frac {3} {5},\:\:\:\frac {7} {8},\:\:\:\frac {8} {9},\:\:\:\frac {7} {10}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's convert all the numbers into decimals.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {7}, \quad \dfrac {2} {3}, \quad \dfrac {3} {5}, \quad \dfrac {7} {8}, \quad \dfrac {8} {9}, \quad \dfrac {7} {10} \\\\
&= 0.714, \quad 0.666, \quad 0.6, \quad 0.875, \quad 0.888, \quad 0.7
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction closest to } 1   &= 0.888 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8} {9}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {8} {9}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's convert all the numbers into decimals.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {7}, \quad \dfrac {2} {3}, \quad \dfrac {3} {5}, \quad \dfrac {7} {8}, \quad \dfrac {8} {9}, \quad \dfrac {7} {10} \\\\
&= 0.714, \quad 0.666, \quad 0.6, \quad 0.875, \quad 0.888, \quad 0.7
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction closest to } 1   &= 0.888 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {8} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {8} {9}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6D88D09C-0FBB-4B9F-8FA7-303284B7A08B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $1 \dfrac {1} {2}$ times $5.6$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5.6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2} \times 5.6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5.6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 2.8} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2}  \times 2.8} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 2.8 \\\\
&= 8.4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8.4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5.6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2} \times 5.6 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5.6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 2.8} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2}  \times 2.8} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 2.8 \\\\
&= 8.4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8.4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

&= 19 - 2 \\\\
&= 17
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D0AE7167-AAFE-44B0-8895-2BD334572908'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I am thinking of two numbers. 

If I double the first and add the second, my answer is $35$. 

If I double the second and subtract the first, my answer is $15$.

By forming and solving two simultaneous equations, find the two numbers.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the first number $= a$

Let the second number $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + b &= 35 \\\\
2b - a &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

First, let's find a

$
\begin{aligned}
2b - a          &= 15 \\\\
2(35 - 2a) - a  &= 15 \\\\
70 - 4a - a    &= 15 \\\\
70 - 5a        &= 15 \\\\
70 -15          &= 5a \\\\
5a             &= 55 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {55} {5} \\\\
a               &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Now, let's find b

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + b &= 35 \\\\
2 \times 11 + b &= 35 \\\\
22 + b &= 35 \\\\
b &= 35 - 22 \\\\
b &= 13
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
<div class='working'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

first number $= 11$, second number $= 13$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

TODOANSWER

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-37279E76-7207-4B08-84B9-07B10F2972FD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Add together $3.7\, metres$ and $64\, cm$ and then subtract $2\, metres$ $7\,cm$. Give your answer in metres.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.

The measurement is given in meters and centimeteres but the answer has to be in meters.

$1 \ \text{m} = 100 \ \text{cm}$

$\dfrac {1} {100} \ \text{m} = 1 \ \text{cm}$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + 64 \ \text{cm} - (2 \ \text {m} + 7 \ \text{cm}) \\\\
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + \dfrac {64} {100} \ \text{m} - (2 \ \text{m} + \dfrac {7} {100} \ \text{m}) \\\\
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + 0.64 \ \text{m}  -  (2 \ \text{m} + 0.07 \ \text{m}) \\\\
&= 4.34 \ \text{m} - 2.07 \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.27 \ \text {m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.27 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_note
The measurement is given in meters and centimeteres but the answer has to be in meters.
<emptyline>
$1 \ \text{m} = 100 \ \text{cm}$
<emptyline>
$\dfrac {1} {100} \ \text{m} = 1 \ \text{cm}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + 64 \ \text{cm} - (2 \ \text {m} + 7 \ \text{cm}) \\\\
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + \dfrac {64} {100} \ \text{m} - (2 \ \text{m} + \dfrac {7} {100} \ \text{m}) \\\\
&= 3.7 \ \text{m} + 0.64 \ \text{m}  -  (2 \ \text{m} + 0.07 \ \text{m}) \\\\
&= 4.34 \ \text{m} - 2.07 \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.27 \ \text {m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.27 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

&= 5 \times 4 \\\\
&= 20
\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If $13! = 13 \times n!$ what number does $'n'$ stand for?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
n!      &= \dfrac {13!} {13} \\\\
n!      &= \dfrac {13 \times 12!} {13} \\\\
n!      &= \dfrac {\cancel {13} \times 12!} {\cancel {13}} \\\\
n!      &= 12! \\\\
n       &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
n!      &= \dfrac {13!} {13} \\\\
n!      &= \dfrac {13 \times 12!} {13} \\\\
n!      &= \dfrac {\cancel {13} \times 12!} {\cancel {13}} \\\\
n!      &= 12! \\\\
n       &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $\frac {14} {20}$ as a **decimal**

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {14} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {14 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {70} {100} \\\\
&= 0.7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {14} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {14 \times 5} {20 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {70} {100} \\\\
&= 0.7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $0.625$ as a **fraction** in its $\underbar {lowest terms}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.625 \\\\
&= \dfrac {625} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 5 \times 5} {25 \times 5 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {5} \times 5} {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {5} \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {5} {8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.625 \\\\
&= \dfrac {625} {1000} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 5 \times 5} {25 \times 5 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {5} \times 5} {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {5} \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {5} {8}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Write the answer to $\frac {13} {20} + 0.1$ as a **percentage** 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + 0.1) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {1} {10}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {10 \times 2}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {2} {20}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13 + 2} {20}) \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {20} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 25 \times 4} {5 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 25 \\\\
&= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$75 \%$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + 0.1) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {1} {10}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {10 \times 2}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13} {20} + \dfrac {2} {20}) \times 100 \\\\
&= ( \dfrac {13 + 2} {20}) \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {20} \times 100 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 100} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 25 \times 4} {5 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 3 \times 25 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 25 \\\\
&= 75 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$75 \%$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Give the ratio 36 : 84 in its **simplest form**

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 36 \ratio 84 \\\\
&= \dfrac {36} {84} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times 3} {12 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 3} {\cancel {12} \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
&= 3 \ratio 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3 \ratio 7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 36 \ratio 84 \\\\
&= \dfrac {36} {84} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times 3} {12 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 3} {\cancel {12} \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \\\\
&= 3 \ratio 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F4652DE7-C2AD-4795-819E-73CC80ABCABB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find the number that is exactly halfway between $6.7$ and $9.1$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {6.7 + 9.1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15.8} {2} \\\\
&= 7.9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7.9$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {6.7 + 9.1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {15.8} {2} \\\\
&= 7.9
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7.9$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If the input is $1$, what is the output?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (1 - 7) \div 2 \\\\
&= -6 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {-6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {-3 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {-3 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= -3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$-3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= (1 - 7) \div 2 \\\\
&= -6 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac {-6} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {-3 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {-3 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= -3
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If the output is $3$, what is the input?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the input = $a$

$
\begin{aligned}
(a - 7) \div 2          &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {a - 7} {2}      &= 3 \\\\
a - 7                   &= 3 \times 2 \\\\
a - 7                   &= 6 \\\\
a                       &= 6 + 7 \\\\
a                       &= 13
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

a                       &= 6 + 7 \\\\
a                       &= 13
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

If the output is $n$, what is the input?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the input = $a$

$
\begin{aligned}
(a - 7) \div 2          &= n \\\\
\dfrac {a - 7} {2}      &= n \\\\
a - 7                   &= n \times 2 \\\\
a - 7                   &= 2n \\\\
a                       &= 2n + 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2n + 7$

</div>

a                       &= 2n + 7
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

Write in figures the number forty-two thousand and eight. How much is this number less than one million? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

The number $= 42008$

$1000000 - 42008 = 957992$

This number is $957992$ less than one million.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$42008 \\\\ 957992$

</div>
</div>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Express $51\, km$ in metres

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{km} &= 1000 \ \text{m} \\\\
1 \times 51 \ \text{km} &= 1000 \times 51 \ \text{m} \\\\
51 \ \text{km} &= 51000 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$51000 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{km} &= 1000 \ \text{m} \\\\
1 \times 51 \ \text{km} &= 1000 \times 51 \ \text{m} \\\\
51 \ \text{km} &= 51000 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$51000 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Express $510\,cm$ in metres

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{cm} &= \dfrac{1}{100} \ \text{m} \\\\
1 \times 510 \ \text{cm} &= \dfrac{1}{100} \times 510 \ \text{m} \\\\
510 \ \text{cm} &= \dfrac{510}{100} \ \text{m} \\\\
510 \ \text{cm} &= 5.10 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5.1 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

1 \times 510 \ \text{cm} &= \dfrac{1}{100} \times 510 \ \text{m} \\\\
510 \ \text{cm} &= \dfrac{510}{100} \ \text{m} \\\\
510 \ \text{cm} &= 5.10 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
